# CPython #140120: Memory leak in `hmac` module with HACL* backend detected by AddressSanitizer

## Metadata

- Issue: [Memory leak in `hmac` module with HACL* backend detected by AddressSanitizer](https://github.com/python/cpython/issues/140120)
- Created: 2025-10-14T15:25:07Z
- Updated: 2025-10-17T03:54:02Z
- Retrieved: 2026-09-10T13:09:26.328206+00:00
- Status: closed; closed: 2025-10-17T03:53:43Z
- Labels: type-bug, performance, extension-modules, 3.14
- Comments: 6

## Fix Data

- Changes: +4 / -0 across 2 file(s)

#### Changed Files

##### `Misc/NEWS.d/next/Security/2025-10-14-18-58-09.gh-issue-140120.WyeECT.rst` (added; +2 / -0)

```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,2 @@
+Fixes a memory leak in ``Hacl_Streaming_HMAC_digest`` by freeing the
+``snd`` and ``thd`` fields of ``tmp_block_state1``.
```

##### `Modules/_hacl/Hacl_Streaming_HMAC.c` (modified; +2 / -0)

```diff
@@ -2378,6 +2378,8 @@ Hacl_Streaming_HMAC_digest(
     Hacl_Agile_Hash_state_s *s11 = tmp_block_state1.snd;
     update_last(s11, prev_len_last, buf_last, r);
     finish0(tmp_block_state1, output);
+    free_(tmp_block_state1.snd);
+    free_(tmp_block_state1.thd);
     return Hacl_Streaming_Types_Success;
   }
   KRML_HOST_EPRINTF("KaRaMeL abort at %s:%d\n%s\n",
```

#### Review Comments

##### `Modules/_hacl/Hacl_Streaming_HMAC.c`

**StanFromIreland**, 2025-10-14T16:01:32Z:

```diff
@@ -2519,4 +2521,3 @@ Hacl_Streaming_HMAC_agile_state
     "unreachable (pattern matches are exhaustive in F*)");
   KRML_HOST_EXIT(255U);
 }
```

````text
```suggestion
}

```
Please revert unrelated changes (line below, I cannot provide a suggestion on the line).
````

#### PR Discussion

**ZeroIntensity**, 2025-10-14T17:06:02Z:

```text
Isn't this vendored code? This should probably be fixed upstream.

cc @picnixz
```

**gpshead**, 2025-10-14T20:23:15Z:

```text
indeed, please report this to https://github.com/hacl-star/hacl-star and @protz or similar will help there.  then we can just update our vendored version pin.
```


### PR #140188: gh-140120: Refresh HACL*

- URL: [https://github.com/python/cpython/pull/140188](https://github.com/python/cpython/pull/140188)
- State: closed; merged: 2025-10-16T05:25:51Z; merge commit: [`3a81313019ba`](https://github.com/python/cpython/commit/3a81313019ba82152653bd86f6ffd87dfe339c60)
- Author: protz
- Changes: +189 / -172 across 32 file(s)

#### Description

```text
This pulls an updated version of HACL* that fixes the memory leak reported in #140120, via an upstream fix.

I did not add a NEWS entry because this seems minor. Thank you.

CC @picnixz and @gpshead 

<!-- gh-issue-number: gh-140120 -->
* Issue: gh-140120
<!-- /gh-issue-number -->
```

```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,2 @@
+Fixed a memory leak in :mod:`hmac` when it was using the hacl-star backend.

```

### PR #140192: [3.14] gh-140120: Refresh HACL* to fix an hmac memory leak (GH-140188)

- URL: [https://github.com/python/cpython/pull/140192](https://github.com/python/cpython/pull/140192)
- State: closed; merged: 2025-10-17T03:53:12Z; merge commit: [`32e60fa220ec`](https://github.com/python/cpython/commit/32e60fa220ec096739f95485f2b62ab950d921e4)
- Author: miss-islington
- Changes: +189 / -172 across 32 file(s)

#### Description

```text
This pulls an updated version of HACL* that fixes the memory leak reported in GH-140120, via an upstream fix.
(cherry picked from commit 3a81313019ba82152653bd86f6ffd87dfe339c60)

Co-authored-by: Jonathan Protzenko <jonathan.protzenko+github@gmail.com>
```

#### Changed Files

```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,2 @@
+Fixed a memory leak in :mod:`hmac` when it was using the hacl-star backend.
+Discovered by ``@ashm-dev`` using AddressSanitizer.
```

## Source Records

Complete verbatim content of the issue body and every comment. Sanitizer stacks, shadow bytes, allocation/free histories, and reproducers are preserved exactly as posted.

### Original Issue

Source: [https://github.com/python/cpython/issues/140120](https://github.com/python/cpython/issues/140120)

````text
# Bug report

### **Bug Description:**

A memory leak has been detected in the `hmac` module when running `test_hmac`. The issue appears to be specific to the `Hacl_Streaming_HMAC` implementation, as indicated by the stack traces from the AddressSanitizer (ASan) report. The leaks occur within functions responsible for HMAC digest computation (`_Py_LibHacl_Hacl_Streaming_HMAC_digest`, `hmac_digest_compute_locked`), suggesting that memory allocated during these operations is not being properly deallocated.

**Steps to Reproduce:**

1.  Configure the build with the following flags to enable debugging and AddressSanitizer:
    ```bash
    CC=clang CXX=clang++ \
    ./configure \
    --disable-optimizations \
    --with-valgrind \
    --with-pydebug \
    --enable-pystats \
    --with-address-sanitizer
    ```

2.  Build CPython:
    ```bash
    make -j$(nproc)
    ```

3.  Run the `hmac` test suite:
    ```bash
    ./python -m test test_hmac
    ```

**System Information:**
*   **OS:** `Ubuntu 25.04 (Plucky Puffin)`
*   **Kernel:** `Linux huawei 6.14.0-33-generic #33-Ubuntu SMP PREEMPT_DYNAMIC Wed Sep 17 23:22:02 UTC 2025 x86_64`
*   **Clang Version:** `Ubuntu clang version 20.1.2 (0ubuntu1)`

**ASan Leak Report:**

[test.log](https://github.com/user-attachments/files/22907996/test.log)

### CPython versions tested on:

CPython main branch

### Operating systems tested on:

Linux

<!-- gh-linked-prs -->
### Linked PRs
* gh-140124
* gh-140188
* gh-140192
<!-- /gh-linked-prs -->
````
